Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The unsupervised operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 480. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 499 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to unsupervised structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 480/499 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
